Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The data_structures operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 720. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 244 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 244. QED.
